##### What was that? Pinterest struggles in Q3, Snapchat's AI search attempt, Meta uses scam ads to fund AI, Discord invests in teen safety, and the chaos of infinite access vs copyright vs government.

This newsletter is supported by our friends at [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/95828b82-8172-4905-931c-672ccb11b49e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the affordable, always-on brand tracking dashboard. 

> Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VaNH!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe2e1fbaf-720d-4dbf-a79e-fea6ad235a33_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/181ceacc-e667-4c36-8ddc-8cfac1c4a95a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Pinterest [reaches](https://substack.com/redirect/3bc73784-44ed-4cde-9ed2-f82fd5da0737?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) 600M users; shares plunges 21%, posts [second-worst day ever](https://substack.com/redirect/cca69e10-5120-470f-b5c8-9db70562b42e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as tariffs drag on ad revenue.
* Meta is earning a fortune on [a deluge of fraudulent ads](https://substack.com/redirect/e4d24261-2272-4127-b4c1-b0348b3a5266?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), documents show.
* Cooked?! Google to offer [Kalshi and Polymarket data](https://substack.com/redirect/df25cc3b-3f11-4adf-a27e-fc8fb0cb7e94?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on finance searches.
	+ Apple to pay Google [$1 billion per year](https://substack.com/redirect/22a47329-4b8f-47ff-a29b-d3637559efae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Siri’s custom Gemini AI model.
* Snap reveals [$400 Million Perplexity deal](https://substack.com/redirect/df4fee2e-024d-4dcb-9696-dae4521b6f2b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and tops [Wall Street expectations](https://substack.com/redirect/f5219d13-14db-413f-9f8b-86a0eaa922bc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Q3.
* Netflix is in talks to license [video podcasts](https://substack.com/redirect/7fbf3e11-cb2c-4045-9b54-682250722575?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from iHeartMedia.
* Discord [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/4ce1543a-6540-4671-a9ff-7120e6fadb36?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) new teen safety features [following](https://substack.com/redirect/02676a95-47ed-4b01-a9c7-049e26bcd76b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) lawsuits.

> *In partnership with [Tracksuit](https://substack.com/redirect/a336f9dc-63c8-48c6-a322-1f6012528d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* 

> Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow *and* [prove](https://substack.com/redirect/a336f9dc-63c8-48c6-a322-1f6012528d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the ROI of brand spend.

> If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.

> [Tracksuit’s](https://substack.com/redirect/a336f9dc-63c8-48c6-a322-1f6012528d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll [get the budget](https://substack.com/redirect/a336f9dc-63c8-48c6-a322-1f6012528d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for the next moderately chaotic idea.

	+ Internet Archive’s [legal fights are over](https://substack.com/redirect/4f3b83af-48f7-409d-9e01-1ba6f5cd8a36?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), but its founder mourns what was lost.

* Google pulls [AI model](https://substack.com/redirect/64e16b0c-80dc-4171-b3bf-5e45c38cbe62?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after senator says it fabricated assault allegation.
* What [a poll of 9,000 adults](https://substack.com/redirect/c3122914-018d-496e-82d3-05090925c651?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reveals about aging in America.
	+ Only 1 in 10 lower-income adults are highly confident they can afford retirement.
* The Trade Desk falls despite [decent Q3](https://substack.com/redirect/4e8ea18c-6160-48b6-8f54-dbe153c9457a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) results.
	+ The Trade Desk tries to [redraw the competitive map](https://substack.com/redirect/ff5bd8a2-4003-4a1b-b482-a64e63bfbc2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with Amazon.
* OpenAI wants [brands to allow their mascots](https://substack.com/redirect/e6f0e0da-f90e-44fb-921e-6e1b362457c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to appear in Gen AI videos.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!sjUU!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa21cfcaf-a721-407c-a9db-778b1aff1e95_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/411206ab-61f5-40ef-96c2-eed36a6dea46?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* France opens [criminal probe](https://substack.com/redirect/bf8a6096-615f-430e-83f6-f0324afb7c99?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) into TikTok over kids’ mental health effects.
* TikTok [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/064d0a90-bb37-47ad-8886-a1d8645d6b03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) its first U.S. creator awards, announces inaugural nominees.
* TikTok Shop is now the [size of eBay](https://substack.com/redirect/4416929d-d1bb-4bbe-86fa-eab75c00a2a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ ‘[It’s organized crime](https://substack.com/redirect/7da7a04d-6d4a-41c5-9cc1-0cd7ebf6d215?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’: TikTok Shop says it’s fighting a new wave of AI scammers.
* How TikTok cozied up to [wealthy investors](https://substack.com/redirect/a5a7821e-e1f0-4b10-90ef-1809e4bf1cd1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Saudi Arabia and the UAE.
* Germany [cracks down](https://substack.com/redirect/2e1d23fb-d09a-4d1d-9730-127af6f9f137?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on ‘TikTok Islamism.’
* [Singapore law](https://substack.com/redirect/df5ea1c3-40ce-4660-8810-4dd94a695173?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) gives it more powers over TikTok, Facebook content.
* TikTok Tennis Creator Network [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/3aeabf4a-685e-4d43-9628-e78a5ad0325a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at Nitto ATP Finals.
* TikTok pushes apps [to broaden](https://substack.com/redirect/7ddfa944-f1cf-442c-b964-077559dd390d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) marketing efforts beyond downloads.
* Turkey fines TikTok [1.75 mln lira](https://substack.com/redirect/50d7864f-8f38-47b8-8a12-47ad933d9552?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for weak data protection measures.
* TikTok expands access to [Bulletin Boards](https://substack.com/redirect/575034f9-1b36-4691-8488-5c2ff4b81f97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for profiles.
* Patria-backed Omnia joins [$9 billion](https://substack.com/redirect/7a669d30-714a-4674-99f2-90ad3fe01613?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) TikTok data center project in Brazil.
* TikTok to face trial in [Nevada](https://substack.com/redirect/771580c3-9ce7-4dc9-9c12-eee82b4f013c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after Supreme Court rejects immunity petition against youth harm lawsuit.
* TikTok is testing [GenAI features](https://substack.com/redirect/ba79670e-1dad-4e06-97c8-72b8e2bf5770?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for videos and photos + creative transitions.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!dE8f!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fc0b0e67a-ad8c-4778-a9ee-8066b4dda5be_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/904dc172-3652-442a-807f-97c8d7ce1732?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Instagram testing [Competitive Insights feature](https://substack.com/redirect/bdcc27aa-6c73-4c20-8515-f2460472a889?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for creators and businesses. You can see follower growth, posting frequency, Reels, and even ads from other users.
* Motion Picture Association issues [cease and desist](https://substack.com/redirect/fcce272d-d854-49b4-ac64-8c783733106f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to Meta: Stop using PG-13 rating.
* Instagram punishes [more](https://substack.com/redirect/7736da69-2038-4542-8f0c-a49ebf470c41?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) creators and pages [discussing](https://substack.com/redirect/12eeba8a-1436-4852-9ec3-ef7536a03012?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) politics and current news with a warning: “Your account/content [can’t be shown](https://substack.com/redirect/af676d92-427a-4271-89c0-1e9d2c8b7ec1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to non-followers.”
* Is Fashion done with Inclusivity, [Instagram](https://substack.com/redirect/d29a8e45-c15a-4bf6-91d0-724b3d44af03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and Kardashians?
* Old men are [better than you](https://substack.com/redirect/a3975e0a-7ae4-4169-902d-60ae3e4bc408?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at Instagram.
* Threads [adds](https://substack.com/redirect/bafec395-67de-4223-8f4f-55743ed4158b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) time management options.
* [Edits App](https://substack.com/redirect/120f105a-08fd-4906-98e0-f7364b17995b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) adds color correction, animations for captions, new video effects and more.

> 😶‍🌫️ Report Break: [61% of 0-2-year-olds](https://substack.com/redirect/aada7d9c-47dc-48d9-82b6-56736bcca138?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) already have tablet access and 56% have smartphones, with a plurality spending 3+ hours on devices and 2-4 hours watching video; 59% even stream in transit.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!VXYk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F54241e92-dc00-45e3-b0a1-422988e5b58f_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/28d54878-4eab-44bb-96c2-cbc5d20b4b92?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Facebook Groups can now [go public](https://substack.com/redirect/e0bcb22b-8bbe-48a4-8202-da3c961504e4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) without exposing members’ private posts.
* [Scam ads](https://substack.com/redirect/696aee73-6818-4027-b6e3-89f67a4ddce4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are flooding social media. These former Meta staffers have a plan.
* Meta [brings](https://substack.com/redirect/bf4cf8d7-7da3-429f-8926-26278487969b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) its short-form video feed of AI slop to Europe.
* India tribunal lifts [WhatsApp data-sharing ban](https://substack.com/redirect/7c506bf0-cd11-4db1-83ca-0b8736bdb614?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), upholds Meta fine.
* Facebook [reintroduces](https://substack.com/redirect/a19f3ed0-51b2-4db2-9143-eb8d27198fca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) itself with its first brand campaign in 4 years.
* Meta accuses [employee’s dad of downloading](https://substack.com/redirect/8e7e0453-217b-4d55-9100-b75a77e026d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) gigantic illegal goon stash.
* Meta rejects French rights [watchdog’s ruling](https://substack.com/redirect/d726ac5c-1e8a-4443-a8e4-06135a8d6e48?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) against algorithm.
	+ Facebook’s [job ads algorithm](https://substack.com/redirect/18b36f58-5a80-4126-87a1-f8b75984704a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is sexist, French equality watchdog rules.
* Why [Meta’s exit from MRC audits](https://substack.com/redirect/f8eecdb7-a652-4ac3-9714-14f16462938b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) should be a wake-up call for marketers.
* Meta reports [improved app promotion performance](https://substack.com/redirect/3610116d-88f8-49c1-8916-239d09d502c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) via AI targeting.
* Meta shares [an update](https://substack.com/redirect/15182466-0c9a-4543-bedb-df474a132043?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on tagging and making AI-generated content easier to detect.
* Meta [still wants](https://substack.com/redirect/2c578500-2060-468e-9aa1-8c90daa32557?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to have its iPhone moment.
* Zuckerberg, Chan shift [bulk of philanthropy](https://substack.com/redirect/f3931d72-20f4-488e-92fd-58054947a2c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to science, focusing on AI and biology to curb disease.
	+ Mark Zuckerberg opened [an illegal school](https://substack.com/redirect/19769472-7ac2-4803-b5a9-910cd1994e01?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) at his Palo Alto compound. His neighbors revolted.
* Meta [details](https://substack.com/redirect/b6d319c7-9c05-432d-b499-13ce312765e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) $600 billion US investment pledge that led to a hot-mic moment between Zuckerberg and Trump.
* Podcast: People are [modding Meta Ray-Bans](https://substack.com/redirect/74286bae-e21b-4cc8-b0f7-b915d4c2f1cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to spy on you.
* [WhatsApp](https://substack.com/redirect/e28316f6-dea7-473d-9f04-35241479f950?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is now available on Apple Watch.
* How an Oregon court became the [stage for a $115,000 showdown](https://substack.com/redirect/3099b96d-3a9e-4495-83a1-950a86ce6be9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) between Meta and Facebook creators.
* Meta opens [flagship store](https://substack.com/redirect/0b7940cb-85c5-4524-b63d-4590c9311de5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in West Hollywood to showcase AI glasses, VR headsets.
* WhatsApp confirms launch of [usernames](https://substack.com/redirect/886fb65d-6bb2-442d-8a8d-69c81d82b70a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2026.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!xdrk!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F504f7135-86f7-4edd-a923-b08f71d3eeb3_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/66cf0b01-1bb7-418e-813e-4abc0b01f5ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* YouTube TV to [offer $20 credit](https://substack.com/redirect/eec52442-85e8-4420-b3aa-1ed1471c476f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to subscribers if contract standoff with ESPN continues: Source.
	+ [No short-term deal](https://substack.com/redirect/9def9084-5d29-457b-b2a3-4c3d45ea3bae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) coming between ESPN-YouTube ahead of ‘MNF.’
* YouTube separates [organic & paid metrics](https://substack.com/redirect/2d628bd1-91df-4c1e-93e2-085fe91fcd46?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in channel analytics.
* YouTube rolls out AI age verification to a [new wave of users](https://substack.com/redirect/2d361c25-7d71-457d-bb66-07285bfeac1c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* YouTube [erased](https://substack.com/redirect/3afb404d-2907-405d-8fba-6b02a4834870?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) 700 videos of Israeli human rights violations.
* Disney-YouTube TV feud extends to [Movies Anywhere](https://substack.com/redirect/76ef5bd8-f541-40d8-8ee0-2c000e4934f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), as Google no longer syncs digital movie purchases.
	+ 24% say they have already canceled or plan to soon.
		- YouTube rep says [cancellation rate](https://substack.com/redirect/9b4eef58-b7d8-404a-aa87-9bec28821c2f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) it has seen ‘does not align with the findings of this survey’
* YouTube locks [sidebar on mobile ads](https://substack.com/redirect/d1cb7b66-b832-44c8-9c2a-3cda064021c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), removing close option.
* Big YouTube channels are [being banned](https://substack.com/redirect/b9ef01e9-1225-4f53-8d45-f07dcb11ba3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). YouTubers are blaming AI.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!SRmL!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F6ddeabac-2fb9-4449-8c6d-6f3b735dce87_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/0751c12e-9f0f-4c6e-8f6c-1705c4e0416a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Google is in [early discussions](https://substack.com/redirect/48d4b739-4302-41e7-b23d-e864a1b43c7f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to deepen its investment in Anthropic.
	+ Google debuts [AI chips](https://substack.com/redirect/732d7ae8-fa48-4f7e-9feb-56df27839009?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with 4X performance boost, secures Anthropic megadeal worth billions.
* Google’s AI Mode gets [new agentic capabilities](https://substack.com/redirect/3313dd91-afee-4711-a46e-0294f3710071?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to help book event tickets and beauty appointments.
* Google AI Overviews drive [61% drop](https://substack.com/redirect/62bf67e1-74ba-49af-9935-6a00ec6c2f13?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in organic CTR, 68% in paid.
* Google Ads pushes ‘[Investment Strategy](https://substack.com/redirect/b5fd4e09-7c82-4e4f-9e62-c7bf24c0e235?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ planning for budget-limited campaigns.
* Google’s [preferred sources tool](https://substack.com/redirect/5266b311-1de8-4b5e-9a49-90c29010a280?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is jammed with spam.
* Google Merchant Center adds [promotions](https://substack.com/redirect/c9c274fa-1f50-4e2f-9e7e-9e97f708469a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for top performing products.

> 🥀 Report Break: An overwhelming majority of teens (84%) described news media with negative words — often characterizing media as intentionally deceptive or invoking negative emotional feelings. > [McKinsey Holiday Spending](https://substack.com/redirect/c090f258-a9e8-4b6f-ae7f-8dd733c99016?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g): The concerns about tariffs have eased, with 24 percent fewer consumers citing them as a primary issue than in the previous quarter.

> Millennials are leading the trend of shopping earlier in the year, with 37 percent starting before October—significantly higher than the 28 percent average across all generations.

> Meanwhile, only 11 percent of consumers surveyed are planning to kick off their shopping during Black Friday weekend. Gen Z stands out as an outlier, with 17 percent still associating Black Friday with the start of their holiday shopping.

* [ClickUp](https://substack.com/redirect/4febc0a2-eb57-4b51-9bc3-cc36a54c65d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) adds new AI assistant to better compete with Slack and Notion.
* Getty Images largely loses [landmark UK lawsuit](https://substack.com/redirect/e065d2ab-a15e-4f98-9d63-0bb40446b9da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over AI image generator.
* Oddest ChatGPT leaks yet: [Cringey chat logs found](https://substack.com/redirect/640ffb61-0dc9-4b64-a7ce-cd1f57af42a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in Google analytics tool.
* OpenAI’s eerily realistic AI video generation app, [Sora](https://substack.com/redirect/6aeed5ff-925f-4fbf-9251-c6ca25429228?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), is now on Android.
	+ As OpenAI hits [1 million business customers](https://substack.com/redirect/3b1fd1fd-512b-426f-85ed-414c3a9a09b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), could the AI ROI tide finally be turning?
* AI song generator Udio offers [brief window for downloads](https://substack.com/redirect/17b2e03e-674e-4a3c-ac88-69f0ea33f7d0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after Universal settlement upsets users.
* Perplexity taps [Lewis Hamilton and Eric André](https://substack.com/redirect/8fadc793-48fe-40f4-9c74-4643278041e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to bring AI to life in comedy short.
* AI GF: POV. ([read](https://substack.com/redirect/1d45aeee-190e-4029-a635-eefd3d295263?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g))
* Nvidia boss defends AI against [claims of bubble](https://substack.com/redirect/7b8c3e9f-6127-4269-9fa4-ca262dc76bef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) by ‘Big Short’ investor.
* Whisper into this [AI-powered smart ring](https://substack.com/redirect/ea4d4bcf-b858-4767-b243-bcba9a50ea01?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to organize your thoughts.
* Laude Institute [announces](https://substack.com/redirect/dcf2b067-f069-4948-a9a5-b921a5f63c09?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) first batch of ‘Slingshots’ AI grants.
* Nvidia-backed Vast Data inks [$1.17 billion AI deal](https://substack.com/redirect/b5bfee92-ffb6-4557-8181-9f46136d0be2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with CoreWeave.
* Federal budget dedicates over $1B to boost [Canadian AI](https://substack.com/redirect/7d10b967-c243-4593-b213-670a3382b937?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and quantum computing.
* OpenAI API [moonlights](https://substack.com/redirect/e4f9ee7d-21ce-4c79-bd76-60ea403a8872?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as malware HQ in Microsoft’s latest discovery.
* Walmart adds [AI-generated audio summaries](https://substack.com/redirect/3dc313b4-540a-4766-81b3-83c616615ecf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to select product pages.

###### PEW: Republicans, Democrats now [equally concerned](https://substack.com/redirect/2bbfa969-96bf-4e2e-a094-06d35d59e75d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) about AI in daily life, but views on regulation differ.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!68dD!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F377b000b-6503-42dc-b88d-e08548e9811c_2336x1182.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/c2ed8d1d-371d-44cf-8115-ad297cdc8eb2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

---

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0cUN!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F79af7349-0432-4bf9-83ad-f76d0fd2a362_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/57ebb7fd-ac87-4093-b12e-9280931d796d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Microsoft forms superintelligence team to rival Meta’s, stresses ‘humanist’ focus.
* LinkedIn will allow more types of [newsworthy content](https://substack.com/redirect/dd1c7e4e-2a9c-45b3-aa12-cf27dc69df74?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [Barry Diller’s IAC](https://substack.com/redirect/cb856af4-ba41-4209-80ce-76eee644f668?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) inks AI deal with Microsoft. That isn’t why the stock is sliding.
* Microsoft brings [AI Search to Copilot](https://substack.com/redirect/ec05e67b-d134-48fd-9538-8d6e30fc91ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with emphasis on citations.
* LinkedIn vows to take more action [against engagement pods](https://substack.com/redirect/0bc9b0bc-9e5c-4956-9dc5-65a88ba413ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Microsoft’s [$15.2B UAE investment](https://substack.com/redirect/e2fa8568-d2e4-4c2f-8400-fe2f0d49f2fd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) turns Gulf State into test case for US AI diplomacy.
* Microsoft to [refund customers](https://substack.com/redirect/c9ba5fa7-6be2-4e41-a656-dd3184a24e88?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over subscription price increase for AI add-on.
* Microsoft built [a fake marketplace](https://substack.com/redirect/3bd93141-050d-4e30-84a5-c9edf3ae3c49?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to test AI agents — they failed in surprising ways.
* Microsoft CEO says [the](https://substack.com/redirect/87c56634-115d-439c-b6e5-aa73c54ab4e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) [company doesn’t have enough electricity](https://substack.com/redirect/87c56634-115d-439c-b6e5-aa73c54ab4e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to install all the AI GPUs in its inventory - ‘you may actually have a bunch of chips sitting in inventory that I can’t plug in’.
* Microsoft, Google say their [data centers create thousands of jobs](https://substack.com/redirect/cca9d71e-ddbc-4f5b-95f5-3e6a2af0b704?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g). Their permit filings say otherwise.
* Cloud startup Lambda [unveils](https://substack.com/redirect/e36f0a8e-7b1f-4ca9-b354-87c859edaf01?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) multi-billion-dollar deal with Microsoft.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!WMjD!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F605790ce-ce9b-44a1-a77e-bc12503caa70_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/4e97f4c0-cf9d-498e-bd70-7e7529634fd2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* New collective brings [direct sales support](https://substack.com/redirect/9a657d11-6825-4858-9825-d3bb098932d8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to independent journalists.
* ITV confirms it is in ‘[preliminary discussions](https://substack.com/redirect/57ad51b6-b641-4e52-980f-6dd8c2aa72c8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to sell Media and Entertainment arm to Sky.
	+ ITV warns of another golden quarter [ad revenue drop](https://substack.com/redirect/f461e288-2988-47bf-9c0a-03ff83fd0862?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* DoubleVerify taps into [IMDb for insights](https://substack.com/redirect/b7f5ea98-9c68-45f5-b2c0-46241bd05c5f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on streaming content.
* [AppLovin](https://substack.com/redirect/1aa6c356-5f9c-458c-8c5e-f519032f977f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shrugs off recent negative headlines with a strong Q3 and self-serve rollout.
* October box office [falls 9%](https://substack.com/redirect/dac2d10e-cb0d-4bb9-a387-91e056dcdd3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) amid dearth of family films.
* Ibotta launches [LiveLift™](https://substack.com/redirect/774b0fe7-a9d8-404b-88a0-14bb8eab9f2a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for CPG brands.
* The Trade Desk and PubMatic are done pretending [Deal IDs](https://substack.com/redirect/3af6aba0-9237-4ada-8d6e-cd1a9764f3ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) work.
* CTV needs to get its act together. ([read](https://substack.com/redirect/b1e6c11f-3557-4a46-b44c-990592d07017?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g))
* [The Trade Desk](https://substack.com/redirect/0dc62eb7-5a65-498d-b596-9266e898e0c1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) swipes at walled gardens in new brand campaign.
* DoubleVerify and [Roku](https://substack.com/redirect/364e17c7-800f-46d8-984b-e5d58c7e42e4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) collaboration strengthens [CTV ecosystem](https://substack.com/redirect/3f285598-4256-45aa-9426-1bc9887be674?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) against fraudulent traffic.
* Two-thirds of listeners say [podcasts recommendations](https://substack.com/redirect/04371bea-26a8-41ee-b39c-5ecdad561655?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) drive purchase decisions.
* Telegraph [revenue and profit flat](https://substack.com/redirect/ced085d8-c8fd-4bf1-9fc3-6d2725adc5da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in 2024 amid ongoing ownership limbo.
* System1 introduces [Test Your Ad Competitive Edge](https://substack.com/redirect/aac18ffb-11be-4839-9fec-c1790f0a927a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), the world’s largest open creative effectiveness database.
* Inside [Bayer’s ad tech and data strategy](https://substack.com/redirect/27118051-c403-4fbd-9443-994250056ea4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to find new customers and prepare for agentic AI.
* Dual income, no kids: What we know about ‘[DINKs](https://substack.com/redirect/d1a49dd3-fae3-420b-b5b9-7449ac98d4fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ in the U.S.
	+ 12% of married couples with at least one spouse in their 30s or 40s have two incomes and no kids.
* ID5 [strengthens](https://substack.com/redirect/dcb79867-bf2a-4aac-931f-8545d21ea6c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) US-foothold with acquisition of TrueData.
* Amperity unveils ‘[Real-Time Profiles](https://substack.com/redirect/39083330-0d63-461d-87ad-df66491d7605?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to enable brands to recognise and respond to customers instantly.
* ESPN [will not let failure push](https://substack.com/redirect/6ad3aeb5-5d70-42dc-9026-f7bfe09d2315?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) it out of the gambling business.

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Amazon sues Perplexity AI over Comet browser’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2f85eacb-e46c-4561-86b3-28ee036d9a60?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">automated shopping and buying</a><span> feature.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Perplexity AI accuses Amazon of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/37113d26-6563-4755-b9bd-6aa6257e9189?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bullying with legal threat</a><span> over Comet browser.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e14f64c0-5f42-4cef-b62a-a88b666cf2e5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The X Effect</a><span>: How the world’s richest man is boosting the British right.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>OpenAI </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7ac16b04-9b2a-4deb-a8dc-677f8f4bcbf1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">walks back</a><span> comments about </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9c923257-c0cb-4958-b4d2-f0b9b18d357d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">government support</a><span> for its AI spending.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/70ceab28-f3e9-46f3-a871-aedb172810f7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Epic and Google</a><span> agree to settle their lawsuit and change Android’s fate globally.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>EU could </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/50c33be3-2b7b-4bc3-8bc0-24bbd63beaa0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">water down AI Act</a><span> amid pressure from Trump and big tech.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/be6728ff-37ff-4f78-b547-518c39bfa4bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Facebook dating</a><span> is a surprise hit for the social network.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Spotify reports </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4eb1ff4f-4e6b-4ce7-8e58-647cb06675fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$669M profit for Q3</a><span> as paying subscribers hit 281 Million.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><em><span>In partnership with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a336f9dc-63c8-48c6-a322-1f6012528d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit</a><span> </span></em></p></blockquote>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Marketers are under the pump to go big or go home. They need to perform, entertain, grow </span><em>and</em><span> </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a336f9dc-63c8-48c6-a322-1f6012528d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">prove</a><span> the ROI of brand spend.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">If you’re trying to take a big bet instead of creating the same cookie cutter campaigns each quarter, you need evidence it improved awareness or shifted perception in your key audience segments — not just reached a million people.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a336f9dc-63c8-48c6-a322-1f6012528d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tracksuit’s </a><span>always-on brand tracking that helps you prove the impact of your boldest campaign work over time…so you’ll </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a336f9dc-63c8-48c6-a322-1f6012528d45?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">get the budget</a><span> for the next moderately chaotic idea.</span></p>
</blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: #e6e6e6;border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Trend Report: Pinterest’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1b81cdcf-a0c3-420e-9c18-575f7ae27c75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Festive Season Edit</a><span> brings precision to festive shopping.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Nvidia’s Jensen Huang says China ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/28964142-1038-4a9f-9e8b-04725f17eb2a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">will win</a><span>’ AI race with US.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta can’t afford its </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1b0cd163-2ae9-4ba0-8f85-c6be163b14ab?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$600B love letter</a><span> to Trump.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Pope Leo presses tech leaders to build </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ea554b0c-ff84-47c9-ac12-7e7b861105fc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI that respects human dignity</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>a16z announces an </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1186fae8-46cc-401f-a2c7-92beeea27ff6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">eight-week new media fellowship</a><span> for creators?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>US Companies announce </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c4b193ba-bb25-4dc1-a81b-10f8a857487b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">most October job cuts</a><span> in over 20 years.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>US holiday sales set to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3384dda7-8ba1-4da5-88fe-d8a2d561e08d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">top $1 trillion</a><span> for the first time, NRF forecasts.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Trade Desk tries to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ff5bd8a2-4003-4a1b-b482-a64e63bfbc2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">redraw the competitive map</a><span> with Amazon.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>OpenAI wants </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e6f0e0da-f90e-44fb-921e-6e1b362457c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">brands to allow their mascots</a><span> to appear in Gen AI videos.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram punishes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7736da69-2038-4542-8f0c-a49ebf470c41?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more</a><span> creators and pages </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/12eeba8a-1436-4852-9ec3-ef7536a03012?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">discussing</a><span> politics and current news with a warning: “Your account/content </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/af676d92-427a-4271-89c0-1e9d2c8b7ec1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">can’t be shown</a><span> to non-followers.”</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Is Fashion done with Inclusivity, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d29a8e45-c15a-4bf6-91d0-724b3d44af03?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instagram</a><span> and Kardashians?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Old men are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a3975e0a-7ae4-4169-902d-60ae3e4bc408?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">better than you</a><span> at Instagram.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bafec395-67de-4223-8f4f-55743ed4158b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">adds</a><span> time management options.</span></p></li>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Facebook Groups can now </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e0bcb22b-8bbe-48a4-8202-da3c961504e4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">go public</a><span> without exposing members’ private posts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/696aee73-6818-4027-b6e3-89f67a4ddce4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Scam ads</a><span> are flooding social media. These former Meta staffers have a plan.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bf4cf8d7-7da3-429f-8926-26278487969b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">brings</a><span> its short-form video feed of AI slop to Europe.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>India tribunal lifts</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7c506bf0-cd11-4db1-83ca-0b8736bdb614?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> WhatsApp data-sharing ban</a><span>, upholds Meta fine.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Facebook </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a19f3ed0-51b2-4db2-9143-eb8d27198fca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reintroduces</a><span> itself with its first brand campaign in 4 years.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta accuses </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8e7e0453-217b-4d55-9100-b75a77e026d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">employee’s dad of downloading</a><span> gigantic illegal goon stash.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Meta rejects French rights </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d726ac5c-1e8a-4443-a8e4-06135a8d6e48?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">watchdog’s ruling</a><span> against algorithm.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Facebook’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/18b36f58-5a80-4126-87a1-f8b75984704a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">job ads algorithm</a><span> is sexist, French equality watchdog rules.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Why </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f8eecdb7-a652-4ac3-9714-14f16462938b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta’s exit from MRC audits</a><span> should be a wake-up call for marketers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta reports</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3610116d-88f8-49c1-8916-239d09d502c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> improved app promotion performance</a><span> via AI targeting.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta shares </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/15182466-0c9a-4543-bedb-df474a132043?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">an update</a><span> on tagging and making AI-generated content easier to detect.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2c578500-2060-468e-9aa1-8c90daa32557?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">still wants</a><span> to have its iPhone moment.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Zuckerberg, Chan shift </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f3931d72-20f4-488e-92fd-58054947a2c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bulk of philanthropy</a><span> to science, focusing on AI and biology to curb disease.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Mark Zuckerberg opened </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/19769472-7ac2-4803-b5a9-910cd1994e01?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">an illegal school</a><span> at his Palo Alto compound. His neighbors revolted.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b6d319c7-9c05-432d-b499-13ce312765e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">details</a><span> $600 billion US investment pledge that led to a hot-mic moment between Zuckerberg and Trump.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Podcast: People are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/74286bae-e21b-4cc8-b0f7-b915d4c2f1cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">modding Meta Ray-Bans</a><span> to spy on you.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e28316f6-dea7-473d-9f04-35241479f950?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">WhatsApp</a><span> is now available on Apple Watch.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>How an Oregon court became the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3099b96d-3a9e-4495-83a1-950a86ce6be9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">stage for a $115,000 showdown</a><span> between Meta and Facebook creators.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta opens </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0b7940cb-85c5-4524-b63d-4590c9311de5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">flagship store</a><span> in West Hollywood to showcase AI glasses, VR headsets.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WhatsApp confirms launch of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/886fb65d-6bb2-442d-8a8d-69c81d82b70a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">usernames</a><span> in 2026. </span></p></li>
</ul>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>YouTube TV to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/eec52442-85e8-4420-b3aa-1ed1471c476f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">offer $20 credit</a><span> to subscribers if contract standoff with ESPN continues: Source.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9def9084-5d29-457b-b2a3-4c3d45ea3bae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">No short-term deal</a><span> coming between ESPN-YouTube ahead of ‘MNF.’</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube separates </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2d628bd1-91df-4c1e-93e2-085fe91fcd46?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">organic &amp; paid metrics</a><span> in channel analytics.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube rolls out AI age verification to a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2d361c25-7d71-457d-bb66-07285bfeac1c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new wave of users</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3afb404d-2907-405d-8fba-6b02a4834870?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">erased</a><span> 700 videos of Israeli human rights violations.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Disney-YouTube TV feud extends to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/76ef5bd8-f541-40d8-8ee0-2c000e4934f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Movies Anywhere</a><span>, as Google no longer syncs digital movie purchases.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;">24% say they have already canceled or plan to soon.</p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube rep says </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9b4eef58-b7d8-404a-aa87-9bec28821c2f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">cancellation rate</a><span> it has seen ‘does not align with the findings of this survey’</span></p></li></ul>
</li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>YouTube locks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d1cb7b66-b832-44c8-9c2a-3cda064021c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sidebar on mobile ads</a><span>, removing close option.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Big YouTube channels are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b9ef01e9-1225-4f53-8d45-f07dcb11ba3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">being banned</a><span>. YouTubers are blaming AI.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🥀 Report Break: An overwhelming majority of teens (84%) described news media with negative words — often characterizing media as intentionally deceptive or invoking negative emotional feelings. </ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c090f258-a9e8-4b6f-ae7f-8dd733c99016?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">McKinsey Holiday Spending</a><span>: The concerns about tariffs have eased, with 24 percent fewer consumers citing them as a primary issue than in the previous quarter.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Millennials are leading the trend of shopping earlier in the year, with 37 percent starting before October—significantly higher than the 28 percent average across all generations.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Meanwhile, only 11 percent of consumers surveyed are planning to kick off their shopping during Black Friday weekend. Gen Z stands out as an outlier, with 17 percent still associating Black Friday with the start of their holiday shopping.</p>
</blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Amazon closes at record after </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a10527f1-b750-4d2b-ac10-f8dfeb3cba7f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$38 billion OpenAI deal</a><span> with AWS.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Metropolis raises </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6bce552d-9906-4b3b-a461-2bfcd06da2a3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$500 million</a><span> for AI parking garages.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Alibaba-backed </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c03a1b2a-a27a-4a23-bd60-699a687d8d0d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Moonshot</a><span> releases its second AI update in four months as China’s AI race heats up.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Salesforce acquires </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/17ae95a4-c46b-4883-bf92-828eedc8792f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Spindle AI</a><span> to bolster analytics for Agentforce.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Chinese EV maker Xpeng to launch </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/60deea04-7db3-4f83-8e28-65ac47cd5dfc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">robotaxis</a><span>, humanoid robots with self-developed AI chips.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Exclusive: China bans </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e644e279-f710-4951-968a-cd13ee04cc53?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">foreign AI chips</a><span> from state-funded data centres, sources say.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AI Agents learn to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7612e303-57df-4df3-b601-d0efad21089f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">haggle prices</a><span> with software vendors.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Denmark eyes new law to protect citizens from </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/46b7b20c-8464-4e74-9889-257b9ce8c337?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI deepfakes</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Fox News falls for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/753b9c56-2b89-4448-bb08-46483b1fc3e8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-generated footage of poor people</a><span> raging about food stamps being shut down, runs false Story that has to be updated with huge correction.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Times is using </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b4bf2551-cebe-46a7-9e34-33d509958816?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI to model synthetic focus groups</a><span> from human audiences.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4febc0a2-eb57-4b51-9bc3-cc36a54c65d2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ClickUp</a><span> adds new AI assistant to better compete with Slack and Notion.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Getty Images largely loses </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e065d2ab-a15e-4f98-9d63-0bb40446b9da?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">landmark UK lawsuit</a><span> over AI image generator.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Oddest ChatGPT leaks yet: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/640ffb61-0dc9-4b64-a7ce-cd1f57af42a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Cringey chat logs found</a><span> in Google analytics tool.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>OpenAI’s eerily realistic AI video generation app, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6aeed5ff-925f-4fbf-9251-c6ca25429228?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sora</a><span>, is now on Android.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>As OpenAI hits </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3b1fd1fd-512b-426f-85ed-414c3a9a09b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">1 million business customers</a><span>, could the AI ROI tide finally be turning?</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AI song generator Udio offers </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/17b2e03e-674e-4a3c-ac88-69f0ea33f7d0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">brief window for downloads</a><span> after Universal settlement upsets users.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>AI GF: POV. (</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1d45aeee-190e-4029-a635-eefd3d295263?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">read</a><span>)</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Nvidia boss defends AI against </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7b8c3e9f-6127-4269-9fa4-ca262dc76bef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">claims of bubble</a><span> by ‘Big Short’ investor.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Whisper into this </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ea4d4bcf-b858-4767-b243-bcba9a50ea01?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-powered smart ring </a><span>to organize your thoughts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Laude Institute </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dcf2b067-f069-4948-a9a5-b921a5f63c09?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">announces</a><span> first batch of ‘Slingshots’ AI grants.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Nvidia-backed Vast Data inks </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b5bfee92-ffb6-4557-8181-9f46136d0be2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$1.17 billion AI deal</a><span> with CoreWeave.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Federal budget dedicates over $1B to boost </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7d10b967-c243-4593-b213-670a3382b937?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Canadian AI</a><span> and quantum computing.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>OpenAI API </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e4f9ee7d-21ce-4c79-bd76-60ea403a8872?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">moonlights</a><span> as malware HQ in Microsoft’s latest discovery.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Walmart adds </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3dc313b4-540a-4766-81b3-83c616615ecf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-generated audio summaries</a><span> to select product pages.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cb856af4-ba41-4209-80ce-76eee644f668?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Barry Diller’s IAC</a><span> inks AI deal with Microsoft. <li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Microsoft to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c9ba5fa7-6be2-4e41-a656-dd3184a24e88?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">refund customers</a><span> over subscription price increase for AI add-on.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Microsoft, Google say their </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cca9d71e-ddbc-4f5b-95f5-3e6a2af0b704?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">data centers create thousands of jobs</a><span>. Their permit filings say otherwise.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Cloud startup Lambda </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e36f0a8e-7b1f-4ca9-b354-87c859edaf01?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unveils</a><span> multi-billion-dollar deal with Microsoft.</span></p></li>
</ul>
